For L-BFGS objective over θ:
- Given θ, compute a=exp(θ - logsumexp θ). For very large θ, use max: m=θ.max(); x=exp(θ-m); a=x/x.sum(). If sum==0 fallback.

- Compute w=e/s. grad_a = _conv_grad(w,a). Return F, grad_theta = a*(grad_a - dot(grad_a,a)).

Let's design L-BFGS function `_lbfqs_curriculum(a, deadline, betas, max_iters, seed)`
Pseudo:
```
def _lbfqs_curriculum(a, deadline, betas=(20,80,300,1000,3000), max_iters=None):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) # ensure no inf; subtract max
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_a(a)
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        # optional reset maxiter
        objective = _make objective? top-level no closures. We can implement a function _lf_objective(theta, beta, n, scale=1.0) top-level.
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(_lf_objective, theta, args=(beta,n), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={...})
        new_a=...
        sc=_score_a(new_a)
        if sc<best_score: update
        # maybe blend best with new? If res failure, keep.
        theta = np.log(new_a+1e-300)
    return best_a,best_score
```
But `_lf_objective` needs return (f, g). We can define top-level `_lbfqs_func(theta, beta, n)`.
Inside, if theta has nan, return (1e100, zeros). For performance, use `np.exp(theta - theta_max)`.

(δ_i,m - e_m) = e_m + w_m - ∑w_i e_m = e_m +w_m - e_m = w_m. For i≠m: w_i-(w_i e_m)=w_i(1-0)=w_i. So h'=w. Great! Thus derivative exactly softmax gradient. `_lbfqs_func`:
```
max_theta = np.max(theta)
ap = np.exp(theta - max_theta)
s = ap.sum()
if s <= 0: return 1e100, zeros
a=ap/s
c=_fft_convolve(a)
cmax=float(np.max(c))
t=beta*(c-cmax)
e=np.exp(t)
ws=e.sum()
F = n*cmax + (n/beta)*np.log(ws) - (n/beta)*np.log(c.size)
w=e/ws
grad_a _conv_grad(w,a)
gtheta = a*(grad_a - np.dot(grad_a,a))
return F, gtheta
```
Potential overflow in exp(t) if beta/c range? t <=0 so safe. Underflow fine.
If `cmax` inf? return inf.

For large beta, w may underflow to only max; if multiple max exact, exp(0) for all max, uniform. Good.

```
opts={'maxiter': max_iter, 'gtol':1e-9, 'ftol':1e-12, 'maxcor':30}
```

Since minimize blocking cannot be interrupted. Set maxiter based on estimated time to stay within. For n=1024, 300 iterations per β x 4=1200. Each eval+grad maybe 0.5ms? Mirror/hard line search design:
```
def _hard_refine(a, deadline, K=16, iters=20000):
    a=normalize; best=a; best_score=score; last=0
    for it in range:
       c=conv(a)
       maxc=c.max()
       if maxc <=0: break
       # weights over top K with temperature 10 relative to gap
       top = argpartition(c, -K); w=0; vals; tlocal=5; w[top]=exp(T*(vals-vals.max()))/sum
       grad=conv_grad(w,a)
       d = grad - mean(grad)
       # We want multiplicative a * exp(-eta*d) (since d approx grad of weighted max; negative)
       # Binary search eta in [1e-6, maybe 10/n?]. Actually d magnitude? Under sum1, d ~? Use eta = n * step0, step0 0.05.
       base_eta = 50.0/n? Let's scale: d_i = grad_i - mean. grad ~2∑w a ~O(1/n). d ~O(1/n). Multiplicative exp(-eta d): to change a by 10%, eta*d ~0.1 => eta ~ n. So choose trial etas = [n/20, n/10, n/5, n/2, n, 2n]
       For each eta, newa=a*exp(-clip(eta*d,-5,5)); normalize; evaluate score; if improve best, accept smallest? choose best among trials. If none improve, reduce eta_max.
       If improvement small, maybe use top K weight softer.
    return best
```

Let's write top-level helpers without nested closures.

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p<<=1
    return p

def _conv_n(n):
    return _next_pow2(2*n-1)

def _corr_n(n):
    return _next_pow2(2*n)

def _fft_convolve(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For small n, okay.

x=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw,N)
return 2.0*x[:n]
```

Param families top-level:
```
def _make_exp_fourier(n, amps, phases, dc=0.0):
    i=np.arange(n)/n
    theta=dc*np.ones(n)
    for m,(amp,ph) in enumerate(zip(amps,phases), start=1):
        theta += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*i + ph)
    a=np.exp(theta-theta.max()); a/=a.sum(); return a
```
But if amps large, underflow zeros; okay.

```
def _resample(a, n):
    arr=_normalize(a)
    m=arr.size
    if m==1: ...
    if m==n: return arr
    if m<n:
       x_old=np.linspace(0, m-1, m)
       x_new=np.linspace(0, n-1, n)
       v=np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    else:
       # downsample by linear interpolation maybe; could average? Interp loses mass; normalize.
       x_old=np.linspace(0, m-1, m)
       x_new=np.linspace(0, n-1, n)
       v=np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    v=np.clip(v,0,1000); norm.
```

```
def _lbfqs_run(a, deadline, betas=None, max_factors=None):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    if a.size <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return a, np.inf
    n=a.size
    if betas is None:
        if n <= 1024: betas=(8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0, 800.0, 2500.0)
        elif n <= 2048: betas=(8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0, 800.0)
        else: betas=(8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_array(a)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    # center theta for numerical: theta -= theta.max()/?? maybe no.
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # maxiter adapts to beta and n
       if beta < 20: maxit = 200
       elif beta < 80: maxit=250
       elif beta < 300: maxit=350
       else: maxit = 500
       # scale maxit down for large n: maxit = max(20, int(maxit * 1200.0 / n)) but not too low
       maxit = int(maxit * (1200.0 / max(n,1)))
       maxit = min(2000, max(30, maxit * (30 if beta>=250 else 1)))
       if _scipy_minimize is not None:
           res = _scipy_minimize(_lbfqs_func, theta, args=(beta,n), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxit, 'maxfun':3*maxit, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxcor':50})
           if res.fun finite:
               newa = _softmax_theta(res.x)
           elif finite res.x: newa=...
           else: newa=theta...
       else:
           newa = a # fallback
       sc=_score_array(newa)
       if sc < best_score: best_a=newa.copy(); best_score=sc; theta=np.log(np.maximum(newa,1e-300))
       # regardless update theta to newa? If newa not better, maybe still improve objective for high beta; using newa as next start may be good even if score worse. But if bad (many zeros), could hurt. We can blend: if sc < 2*best maybe use newa; else keep best? For curriculum, final beta wants optimize high beta; maybe start from best score but high beta might need further. Use newa if finite and not too bad (score < best_score*1.5). Else keep best.
       if np.all(np.isfinite(newa)) and sc < best_score*1.5:
           theta=np.log(np.maximum(newa,1e-300))
       else:
           theta=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-300))
    return best_a,best_score
```

Let's write:
```
def _fft_convolve(a):
   N=_pow2_geq(2*a.size-1); return _fft_convolve_N(a,N)
def _fft_convolve_N(a,N):
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N); return c[:2*a.size-1]
def _conv_grad(w,a):
   N=_pow2_geq(2*a.size); return _conv_grad_N(w,a,N)
def _conv_grad_N(w,a,N):
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N); x=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw,N); return 2.0*x[:a.size]
```
For w length L=2n-1, if N=pow2(2n) > L; okay. If w length>N? no. But for n small, N=next_pow2(2n) may be less than L? n=1: L=1,N=2 >1. Good.

`_lbfqs_func`:
```
def _lbfqs_func(theta, beta, n, Nc, Ng):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if th.shape[0] != n: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    m = float(np.max(th))
    x = np.exp(th - m)
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sx):
         return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    a = x / sx
    c = _fft_convolve_N(a, Nc)
    if c.size == 0: return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    t = beta * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(t)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
         w = np.zeros(c.size); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
         w = e / se
    F = n*cmax + (n/beta)*np.log(se) - (n/beta)*np.log(float(c.size))
    ga = _conv_grad_N(w, a, Ng)
    gtheta = a * (ga - float(np.dot(ga, a)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)): return 1e100, np.zeros(n)
    return float(F), gtheta
```
Check F can be negative due subtract logL; okay. It may be negative if cmax small; no issue.

At high beta, `se` may be 1 if only max. F ≈n*cmax. grad w one-hot. Good.

No. We can use `x = np.exp(np.clip(th-m, -745, 0))` to keep underflow? Exp(-1000)=0. We might want avoid exact zeros by using lower bound: after softmax, set `a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)` and renormalize. But if L-BFGS expects smooth, modifying a in objective creates inconsistent gradient? It is still a smooth-ish mapping if we define a_i=max(exp(θ_i-m),ε)/sum. Gradient different but can be handled? def _lp_polish(a, deadline, max_iters=30, blend=0.4):
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size; best=a; best_score=score
    if _scipy_minimize? Actually linprog from scipy.optimize import linprog. Need import at top.
    try from scipy.optimize import linprog
    except: linprog=None
    if linprog is None: return best,best_score
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       if n>2048: maybe skip? or do once.
       ap = (1-1e-8)*a + 1e-8/n
       A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
       for i in range(2*n-1):
          start=max(0,i-n+1); end=min(n-1,i)
          if start<=end:
             seg=ap[i-start : i-end+1][::-1]
             A[i,start:end+1]=seg
       # normalize A? constraints <=1, A entries sum per row maybe not uniform; okay.
       cvec=-np.ones(n)
       res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
          y=res.x; s=y.sum()
          if s>1e-12:
             g=y/s
             new=(1-blend)*a+blend*g
             sc=score(new)
             if sc<best_score:
                best_a, best_score = new, sc
                # maybe do a short L-BFGS on new? optional
             else:
                # try smaller blend
                for t in [0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8]:
                   new=(1-t)*a+t*g; sc=score; if sc<best: update
                else break? skip
       else: break
       a=best_a.copy()
    return best_a,best_score
```

    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _scipy_linprog = None

_POW2_CACHE = {}

def _pow2_geq(m):
    val = _POW2_CACHE.get(int(m))
    if val is None:
        v = 1
        mm = int(m)
        while v < mm:
            v <<= 1
        _POW2_CACHE[mm] = v
    return v
```
But `m` can be numpy int? Use int. Dict with int keys.

`_normalize_sequence`:
```
def _normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / arr.size
    return arr / s
```
But if input sum small, we set uniform; if arr size 0, returns length1.
Actually if sum <=0.01, original evaluate inf; we can return uniform. But if all zeros length n uniform sum1. Good.

`_score_array`:
```
def _score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=arr.size
    if n==0: return inf
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr,0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <0.01: return inf
    arr=arr/s # normalize? Score scale invariant but to avoid large. Actually sum in formula. If normalize to 1, score=2n maxc.
    N=_pow2_geq(2*n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(arr,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: return inf
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return inf
    return 2.0*n*mx
```
Since normalized sum1. If arr sum originally not 1, normalize. This matches evaluate after security. Good.

But if arr has negative clipped? okay.

def _soft_mirror(a, deadline, K=0, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=300.0):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_array(a)
    if n==0 or time.monotonic()>deadline: return best_a,best_score
    it=0; scale=30.0; last_imp=it
    max_iter=200000
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<max_iter:
       it+=1
       if it%100==0:
          c=_fft_convolve(a); mx=c.max(); # unchanged? No need
       c=_fft_convolve(a)
       mx=float(c.max())
       if not finite: break
       if K>0:
           if c.size<=K: idx=np.arange(c.size)
           else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
           local_betas = 5.0? For top, use w=exp(5*(c[idx]-mx))/sum
           w=np.zeros(c.size)
           vals=c[idx]-mx
           e=np.exp(np.clip(vals*10? ...));
           w[idx]=e/e.sum()
       else:
           beta=beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*min(1.0,it/2000.0)
           t=c-mx
           e=np.exp(beta*t)
           se=e.sum()
           w=e/se if se>0 else ones
       ga=_conv_grad(w,a)
       d=ga - np.mean(ga)
       dnorm=np.std(d)
       if dnorm < 1e-15:
           # perturb
           rng = np.random.RandomState(it)
           d = rng.standard_normal(n) - mean
           dnorm=1
       # trial scale from scale
       s=scale
       improved=False
       for _ in range(8):
           e=np.clip(-s*d, -4.0,4.0)
           newa=a*np.exp(e)
           newa=np.where(np.isfinite(newa), newa,0.0)
           newa=_normalize_sequence(newa)
           sc=_score_array(newa)
           if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_a=newa.copy(); best_score=sc; a=newa; last_imp=it; scale=min(scale*1.1, 500.0); improved=True; break
           elif sc < _score_array(a)*1.01: # improve current? But we didn't store current_score. Use current_score=2n*mx.
               current_score = 2*n*mx
           s*=0.5
       if not improved:
           scale=max(0.5*scale, 1.0)
           if scale < 1.0? break maybe
       if it-last_imp > 5000:
           a=best_a.copy(); scale=30.0; it=last_imp? Need patience.
    return best_a,best_score
```

def _optimize_bucket(bucket, hard, reserve, max_count, base_alloc, seed):
    out=[]
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    n_items=len(bucket[:max_count])
    for i in range(n_items):
       if time.monotonic() + reserve >= hard: break
       remaining=hard - time.monotonic() - reserve
       if remaining <= 0: break
       alloc = remaining / max(1, n_items - i)
       alloc = max(15.0, min(base_alloc, alloc))
       tl = time.monotonic() + alloc
       print(...)
       a_opt,s_opt = _run_candidate(bucket[i], tl, seed+1000*i)
       out.append((a_opt,s_opt))
    return out
```

def _run_candidate(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a0=_normalize_sequence(a); s0=_score_array(a0)
    best_a=a0.copy(); best_score=s0
    if _scipy_minimize is not None:
       a1,s1=_lbfqs_run(a0, deadline, seed=seed+1)
       if s1<best_score: update; a_best=a1
    else:
       a1,s1=_soft_mirror(a0, deadline)
    # evaluate blends of initial and result
    for t in (0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9):
       blend=(1.0-t)*a0 + t*a1
       blend=_normalize_sequence(blend)
       sc=_score_array(blend)
       if sc<best_score: update
    return best_a,best_score
```

Let's write `_soft_mirror` concise but correct:
```
def _soft_mirror(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a, np.inf
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_array(a)
    if _scipy_minimize? no matter
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+7)
    scale=20.0
    patience=2000
    it=0; last=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<200000:
       it+=1
       c=_fft_convolve(a)
       if c.size==0: break
       mx=float(c.max())
       if not np.isfinite(mx): break
       # weights top 8
       K = min(8, c.size)
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       vals=c[idx]
       local_beta=10.0
       e=np.exp(local_beta*(vals-mx))
       se=e.sum()
       if se<=0: w uniform? else
       w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=e/se
       ga=_conv_grad(w,a)
       d=ga - np.mean(ga)
       if float(np.std(d)) < 1e-15:
           d = rng.standard_normal(n); d -= d.mean()
       curr_score=2.0*n*mx
       step=scale
       improved=False
       for _ in range(6):
           eout=np.clip(-step*d, -3.0, 3.0)
           newa=a*np.exp(eout)
           newa=np.where(np.isfinite(newa), newa,0.0)
           newa=_normalize_sequence(newa)
           sc=_score_array(newa)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < curr_score*(1.0-1e-12):
               a=newa; curr_score=sc
               if sc < best_score:
                   best_score=sc; best_a=newa.copy(); last=it
               # accept, maybe increase step if best improved
               if sc < best_score + 1e-12:
                   scale = min(100.0, scale*1.05)
               improved=True
               break
           step *= 0.5
       if not improved:
           scale *= 0.7
           if scale < 0.1:
               a=best_a.copy()
               scale=20.0
           if it-last > patience:
               noise = rng.standard_normal(n)*0.005/a.size? 
               a=(1-0.01)*best_a + 0.01*noise? better:
               pert = best_a + rng.standard_normal(n)*0.01*best_a.mean()
               a=_normalize_sequence(np.clip(pert,0,None))
               patience=max(500, int(patience*1.2))
    return best_a,best_score
```

LP polish top-level:
```
def _lp_polish(a, deadline, max_iters=5):
   a=_normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_score_array(a)
   if _scipy_linprog is None or n<=0 or n>1024: return best
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
      ap = (1.0 - 1e-8)*a + 1e-8/n
      L=2*n-1
      A=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
      for i in range(L):
         start = 0 if i < n-1 else i-n+1
         end = n-1 if i >= n-1 else i
         if start <= end:
             vals=ap[i-start : i-end + 1][::-1]
             A[i, start:end+1]=vals
      # Normalize rows? Maybe not.
      cvec=-np.ones(n)
      try:
         bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
         res=_scipy_linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(L), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
      except Exception:
         break
      if not res.success: break
      y=res.x
      y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
      y=np.clip(y,0,1000)
      s=float(y.sum())
      if s <= 1e-12: break
      g=y/s
      improved=False
      for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9):
         newa=(1-t)*a + t*g
         newa=_normalize_sequence(newa)
         sc=_score_array(newa)
         if sc < best_score:
             best_a=newa.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True
      if not improved:
         break
      a=best_a.copy()
   return best_a,best_score
```
Potential: A can be large for n=1024; okay. `bounds` list of tuples.

```
def _make_exp_fourier(n, freqs, amps, phases=(), dc=0.0):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n, endpoint=False)
    theta=np.full(n, dc)
    for f,amp,ph in zip(freqs,amps,phases):
       theta += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t + ph)
    m=np.max(theta)
    a=np.exp(theta - m)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    return _normalize_sequence(a)
```
If all theta -inf? no. Frequency maybe 0? If f=0, sin const, okay. `freqs` list int.

def _lbfqs_run(a, deadline, betas=None, max_iter_scale=1.0, seed=0):
    a0=_normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a0.size
    best_a=a0.copy()
    best_score=_score_array(a0)
    if n==0: return best_a,best_score
    deadline=float(deadline)
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        a_m, s_m = _soft_mirror(a0, deadline, seed=seed)
        if s_m < best_score: return a_m, s_m
        return best_a,best_score
    N=_pow2_geq(2*n)
    if betas is None: ...
    current_a=a0.copy()
    theta=_theta_from_a(current_a)
    rng=np.random.RandomState((seed + 12345) % (2**32 - 1))
    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       if bi > 0:
           theta = _theta_from_a(current_a)
           noise_scale = 1e-5 if beta > 200 else 1e-4
           theta = theta + noise_scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
       if beta < 20: reg=2e-3
       elif beta < 100: reg=5e-4
       elif beta < 400: reg=5e-5
       elif beta < 1500: reg=1e-6
       else: reg=2e-7
       # maxit
       if n<=1024: base=300
       elif n<=2048: base=240
       elif n<=4096: base=190
       else: base=140
       if beta < 20: factor=0.7
       elif beta < 100: factor=1.0
       elif beta < 400: factor=1.5
       else: factor=2.2
       maxit=int(base*factor*max_iter_scale)
       maxit=max(40, min(3500, maxit))
       try:
          res=_scipy_minimize(_lbfqs_func, theta, args=(beta,n,N,reg), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B',
                              options={'maxiter':maxit,'maxfun':3*maxit,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-10,'maxcor':50})
          if res.x is not None and res.x.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
              newa=_softmax_theta(res.x)
          else:
              newa=current_a.copy()
       except Exception:
          newa=current_a.copy()
       newa=_normalize_sequence(newa)
       sc=_score_array(newa)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_a=newa.copy()
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score + 0.03:
          current_a=newa.copy()
       else:
          current_a=best_a.copy()
    # one final low noise high beta? No.
    return best_a,best_score
```

Implementation of `_optimize_bucket`:
```
def _optimize_bucket(bucket, hard, reserve, max_count, base_alloc, seed):
    out=[]
    n_items=min(max_count, len(bucket))
    for i in range(n_items):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard - reserve:
            break
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - reserve
        if remaining <= 0:
            break
        alloc = remaining / float(n_items - i)
        if alloc > base_alloc:
            alloc = base_alloc
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(bucket[i], dl, seed=seed + 1000*i)
        out.append((a_opt,s_opt))
    return out
```
This uses future time not nested. Good.

But if `remaining` large and base_alloc 120, alloc 120; if many remaining, total may exceed reserve? It uses base_alloc cap; okay. If many, near reserve stops.

```
def _soft_mirror(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return a, inf
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score = _score_array(a)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return best_a,best_score
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    scale = max(20.0, 0.1*n)
    K = min(16, 2*n-1)
    it=0; last=it; patience=3000; max_iter=100000
    while ...:
       it +=1
       c=_fft_convolve(a)
       mx=float(np.max(c))
       if not finite: break
       if c.size <= K: idx=arange
       else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       vals=c[idx]
       local_beta = 30.0  # maybe adapt: 10 + it/5
       e=np.exp(local_beta*(vals-mx)); se=e.sum()
       if se <=0: w[idx]=1/K
       else w[idx]=e/se
       ga=_conv_grad(w,a)
       d=ga - np.mean(ga)
       dstd=float(np.std(d))
       if dstd < 1e-14:
          d = rng.standard_normal(n); d -= d.mean(); dstd=1
       curr_score=2.0*n*mx
       s=scale
       improved=False
       for _ in range(6):
          expo = -s*d
          expo = np.clip(expo, -3.0, 3.0)
          newa = a * np.exp(expo)
          newa=np.where(np.isfinite(newa), newa, 0.0)
          newa=_normalize_sequence(newa)
          sc=_score_array(newa)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < curr_score - 1e-12:
             a=newa; curr_score=sc
             if sc < best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_a=newa.copy(); last=it
             scale = min(1.0*n, scale*1.05)
             improved=True
             break
          s *=0.5
       if not improved:
          scale *=0.6
          if scale < max(1.0, 0.01*n):
             a=best_a.copy()
             scale=max(20.0,0.1*n)
       if it - last > patience:
          noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * (0.01 / max(1.0, float(n))?)
```
Noise: Since a_i ~1/n, noise scale 0.01/n? If add normal with std 0.01/n times maybe. `noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * (0.01 / n)` then a = a + noise; normalize. Or multiply a: `pert = a + (0.005 * a.mean()) * rng.standard_normal(n)`; a.mean=1/n, std 0.005/n. Good.

          pert = best_a + (0.01 * best_a.mean()) * rng.standard_normal(n)

Detailed code:

```
def _lbfqs_func(theta, beta, n, N, reg=0.0):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if th.size != n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(th))
    x = np.exp(th - m)
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = x / sx
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = beta * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(t)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / se
    F = n * cmax + (n / beta) * float(np.log(se)) - (n / beta) * float(np.log(float(c.size)))
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, N)
    ga = 2.0 * corr[:n]
    inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)
    if reg > 0.0:
        d = a - inv_n
        F += 0.5 * reg * float(n) * float(np.dot(d, d))
        ga = ga + reg * float(n) * d
    gm = float(np.dot(ga, a))
    gtheta = a * (ga - gm)
    gtheta = gtheta - float(np.mean(gtheta))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(F), gtheta
```
Check: `np.exp(t)` where t <=0; if beta huge and c-cmax small, okay. If `cmax` negative? Conv nonnegative but FFT tiny negative; cmax nonnegative.

If beta is 0? We never pass 0.

`F` could be negative. fine.

`_run_candidate` code:
```
def _run_candidate(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a0 = _normalize_sequence(a)
    s0 = _score_array(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy(); best_score = s0
    tl = time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= tl + 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score
    if _scipy_minimize is not None:
       remaining = deadline - tl
       lb_deadline = tl + 0.75 * remaining
       if lb_deadline < tl + 20.0: lb_deadline = deadline
       a1,s1 = _lbfqs_run(a0, lb_deadline, seed=seed)
       if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score: best_score=s1; best_a=a1.copy()
       # blends
       a1 = _normalize_sequence(a1)
       for tt in (0.3,0.5,0.75,0.9):
           blend=(1.0-tt)*a0 + tt*a1
           blend=_normalize_sequence(blend)
           sc=_score_array(blend)
           if sc < best_score: ...
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
           a2,s2 = _soft_mirror(best_a, deadline, seed=seed+5)
           if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score: update
    else:
       a1,s1 = _soft_mirror(a0, deadline, seed=seed)
       if ... update
    return best_a,best_score
```
If `a1` from `_lbfqs_run` is None? no.

`_soft_mirror` uses top-K and may take time; in `_run_candidate`, after L-BFGS there is remaining maybe 25%.

Now, `_optimize_bucket` prints? Avoid too many. In `_optimize_bucket`, print starting? It doesn't know best. Could print i,n. Fine.

```
def _optimize_bucket(bucket, hard, reserve, max_count, base_alloc, seed):
    out=[]
    n_items=min(max_count, len(bucket))
    print(f"  optimizing bucket of {n_items} candidates...", flush=True)
    for i in range(n_items):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard - reserve: break
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - reserve
        if remaining <= 30.0: break
        alloc = remaining / float(n_items - i)
        if alloc > base_alloc: alloc=base_alloc
        if alloc < 10.0: break
        a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(bucket[i], time.monotonic()+alloc, seed=seed+100*i)
        out.append((a_opt,s_opt))
        print(f"    bucket {i} n={a_opt.size} score={s_opt:.8f}", flush=True)
    return out
```

```python
"""
Low-peak nonnegative step-function optimization.

The objective is scale-invariant.  The sequence is treated as a probability
vector.  The main optimizer is a multi-stage L-BFGS search over
a=softmax(theta).  The smooth objective is a smoothed maximum of the
linear self-convolution:

    n * max_k c_k + small log-sum-exp correction + small uniform-L2 anchor.

Curriculum annealing over the smoothing parameter, multiple starts,
upsampled refinements, a top-peak multiplicative mirror polish, and an
optional small LP-direction polish are used to squeeze out local losses.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _scipy_linprog
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None
    _scipy_linprog = None

_POW2_CACHE = {}


def _pow2_geq(m):
    m = int(m)
    v = _POW2_CACHE.get(m)
    if v is None:
        p = 1
        while p < m:
            p <<= 1
        v = p
        _POW2_CACHE[m] = v
    return v


def _normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def _softmax_theta(theta):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = th.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    m = float(np.max(th))
    x = np.exp(th - m)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return x / s


def _theta_from_a(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-300)
    th = np.log(arr)
    th -= float(np.max(th))
    return th


def _fft_convolve(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = _pow2_geq(2 * n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(arr, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _conv_grad(w, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = _pow2_geq(2 * n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, N)
    return 2.0 * corr[:n]


def _score_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    arr = arr / s
    c = _fft_convolve(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mx


def _resample(a, n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    arr = _normalize_sequence(a)
    m = arr.size
    if m == 1:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[0] = 1.0
        return out
    if m == n:
        return arr.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), m)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, float(n - 1), n)
    out = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize_sequence(out)


def _endpoint_tent(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 0.5 * (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * t - 1.0))
    return _normalize_sequence(a)


def _make_linear_sine(n, amp, phase):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (t + float(phase)))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize_sequence(a)


def _make_exp_fourier(n, freqs, amps, phases, dc=0.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    theta = np.full(n, float(dc), dtype=np.float64)
    for f, amp, ph in zip(freqs, amps, phases):
        f = int(f)
        if f == 0:
            theta += float(amp) * float(np.sin(float(ph)))
        else:
            theta += float(amp) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * t + float(ph))
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(theta - m)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize_sequence(a)


def _lbfqs_func(theta, beta, n, N, reg=0.0):
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if th.size != n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(th))
    x = np.exp(th - m)
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = x / sx

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    t = beta * (c - cmax)
    e = np.exp(t)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / se

    F = n * cmax + (n / beta) * float(np.log(se)) - (n / beta) * float(np.log(float(c.size)))

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, N)
    ga = 2.0 * corr[:n]

    inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)
    if reg > 0.0:
        d = a - inv_n
        F += 0.5 * reg * float(n) * float(np.dot(d, d))
        ga = ga + reg * float(n) * d

    ga_dot_a = float(np.dot(ga, a))
    gtheta = a * (ga - ga_dot_a)
    gtheta -= float(np.mean(gtheta))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gtheta)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(F), gtheta


def _lbfqs_run(a, deadline, betas=None, max_iter_scale=1.0, seed=0):
    a0 = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a0.size
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = _score_array(a0)
    if n == 0:
        return best_a, best_score

    deadline = float(deadline)
    if _scipy_minimize is None:
        a_m, s_m = _soft_mirror(a0, deadline, seed=seed)
        if np.isfinite(s_m) and s_m < best_score:
            return a_m, s_m
        return best_a, best_score

    N = _pow2_geq(2 * n)
    if betas is None:
        if n <= 1024:
            betas = (8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0, 800.0)
        elif n <= 2048:
            betas = (8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0)
        elif n <= 4096:
            betas = (8.0, 24.0, 80.0, 250.0)
        else:
            betas = (12.0, 36.0, 120.0)

    current_a = a0.copy()
    theta = _theta_from_a(current_a)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int((int(seed) + 12345) % (2**31 - 1)))

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        if bi > 0:
            theta = _theta_from_a(current_a)
            noise_scale = 1e-5 if beta > 200.0 else 1e-4
            theta = theta + noise_scale * rng.standard_normal(n)

        if beta < 20.0:
            reg = 2e-3
        elif beta < 100.0:
            reg = 5e-4
        elif beta < 400.0:
            reg = 5e-5
        elif beta < 1500.0:
            reg = 1e-6
        else:
            reg = 2e-7

        if n <= 1024:
            base = 300
        elif n <= 2048:
            base = 240
        elif n <= 4096:
            base = 190
        else:
            base = 140

        if beta < 20.0:
            factor = 0.7
        elif beta < 100.0:
            factor = 1.0
        elif beta < 400.0:
            factor = 1.5
        else:
            factor = 2.2

        maxit = int(base * factor * float(max_iter_scale))
        maxit = max(40, min(3500, maxit))

        new_a = current_a.copy()
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _lbfqs_func,
                theta,
                args=(float(beta), int(n), int(N), float(reg)),
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxit),
                    "maxfun": int(3 * maxit + 1),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxcor": 50,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None and np.asarray(res.x).size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                new_a = _softmax_theta(res.x)
        except Exception:
            new_a = current_a.copy()

        new_a = _normalize_sequence(new_a)
        sc = _score_array(new_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = new_a.copy()
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score + 0.03:
            current_a = new_a.copy()
        else:
            current_a = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _soft_mirror(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_array(a)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int((int(seed) + 7) % (2**31 - 1)))
    scale = max(20.0, 0.1 * float(n))
    K = min(16, 2 * n - 1)
    it = 0
    last_imp = 0
    patience = 3000
    max_iter = 100000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iter:
        it += 1
        c = _fft_convolve(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        mx = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(mx):
            break

        if c.size <= K:
            idx = np.arange(c.size)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        vals = c[idx]
        eb = np.exp(30.0 * (vals - mx))
        se = float(eb.sum())
        if se <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        else:
            w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            w[idx] = eb / se

        ga = _conv_grad(w, a)
        d = ga - float(np.mean(ga))
        dstd = float(np.std(d))
        if dstd < 1e-14:
            d = rng.standard_normal(n)
            d -= float(np.mean(d))

        curr_score = 2.0 * float(n) * mx
        s = scale
        improved = False

        for _ in range(6):
            expo = -s * d
            expo = np.clip(expo, -3.0, 3.0)
            new_a = a * np.exp(expo)
            new_a = np.where(np.isfinite(new_a), new_a, 0.0)
            new_a = _normalize_sequence(new_a)
            sc = _score_array(new_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < curr_score - 1e-12:
                a = new_a
                curr_score = sc
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = new_a.copy()
                    last_imp = it
                scale = min(1.0 * float(n), scale * 1.05)
                improved = True
                break
            s *= 0.5

        if not improved:
            scale *= 0.6
            if scale < max(1.0, 0.01 * float(n)):
                a = best_a.copy()
                scale = max(20.0, 0.1 * float(n))

        if it - last_imp > patience:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n) * (0.01 * float(np.mean(best_a)))
            pert = best_a + noise
            a = _normalize_sequence(np.clip(pert, 0.0, None))
            patience = int(min(20000, patience * 1.3))

    return best_a, best_score


def _lbfqs_polish(a, deadline, seed=0):
    return _lbfqs_run(a, deadline, betas=(300.0, 900.0, 2500.0),
                      max_iter_scale=0.7, seed=seed)


def _run_candidate(a, deadline, seed=0):
    a0 = _normalize_sequence(a)
    s0 = _score_array(a0)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_score = s0
    t = time.monotonic()
    if deadline <= t + 5.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    if _scipy_minimize is not None:
        remaining = float(max(0.0, deadline - t))
        lb_deadline = t + 0.75 * remaining
        if lb_deadline < t + 20.0:
            lb_deadline = deadline
        a1, s1 = _lbfqs_run(a0, lb_deadline, seed=int(seed))
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best_a = a1.copy()

        a1 = _normalize_sequence(a1)
        for mix in (0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
            blend = (1.0 - mix) * a0 + mix * a1
            blend = _normalize_sequence(blend)
            sc = _score_array(blend)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = blend.copy()

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
            a2, s2 = _soft_mirror(best_a, deadline, seed=int(seed) + 5)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_a = a2.copy()
    else:
        a1, s1 = _soft_mirror(a0, deadline, seed=int(seed))
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best_score = s1
            best_a = a1.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _optimize_bucket(bucket, hard, reserve, max_count, base_alloc, seed):
    out = []
    n_items = min(int(max_count), len(bucket))
    if n_items <= 0:
        return out
    print("    bucket: %d candidates" % n_items, flush=True)
    for i in range(n_items):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard - reserve:
            break
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - reserve
        if remaining <= 30.0:
            break
        alloc = remaining / float(n_items - i)
        if alloc > base_alloc:
            alloc = base_alloc
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(bucket[i], dl, seed=int(seed) + 1000 * i)
        out.append((a_opt, s_opt))
        print("      bucket %d: n=%d score=%.8f" % (i, int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)
    return out


def _lp_polish(a, deadline, max_iters=5):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_array(a)

    if _scipy_linprog is None or n <= 0 or n > 1024:
        return best_a, best_score

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        ap = (1.0 - 1e-8) * a + 1e-8 / float(n)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(L):
            start = i - n + 1
            if start < 0:
                start = 0
            end = n - 1 if i >= n - 1 else i
            for j in range(start, end + 1):
                A[i, j] = ap[i - j]

        try:
            bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
            res = _scipy_linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if not res.success:
            break
        y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
        y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sy = float(y.sum())
        if sy <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = y / sy
        improved = False
        for mix in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
            blend = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * g
            blend = _normalize_sequence(blend)
            sc = _score_array(blend)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = blend.copy()
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        a = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _as_list(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        arr = np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
        s = 1.0
    return [float(x) for x in arr / s]


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    np.random.seed(int(seed))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    base_a = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            seq = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
            arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size > 0 and float(arr.sum()) > 0.01:
                base_a = arr
                print("[init] Using height_sequence_1 length %d" % int(arr.size), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            base_a = None

    lengths = []
    for target in (1024, 2048, 3072, 4096):
        lengths.append(int(target))
    if base_a is not None:
        m = int(base_a.size)
        if 256 <= m <= 8192 and m not in lengths:
            lengths.append(m)
    lengths = lengths[:4]
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [1024]

    pool = []
    counter = 0

    def add_pool_candidate(arr):
        nonlocal_zero = None
        return nonlocal_zero

    # The assignment to nonlocal cannot be used; avoid nested helper entirely.
    # Build pool with explicit loops below.
    if base_a is not None:
        for n in lengths[:3]:
            a = _resample(base_a, n)
            s = _score_array(a)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
                counter += 1

    for n in lengths[:3]:
        a = _endpoint_tent(n)
        s = _score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
            counter += 1

    for n in lengths[:2]:
        for amp in (0.5, 0.75, 0.9, 1.1):
            for phase in (0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
                a = _make_linear_sine(n, amp, phase)
                s = _score_array(a)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
                    counter += 1

    for amp in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for phase in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
            a = _make_exp_fourier(lengths[0], [1], [amp], [phase], 0.0)
            s = _score_array(a)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
                counter += 1

            if len(lengths) > 1:
                a = _make_exp_fourier(lengths[1], [1], [amp], [phase], 0.0)
                s = _score_array(a)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
                    counter += 1

    for r in range(60):
        if time.monotonic() > hard - 1500.0:
            break
        n = lengths[r % min(len(lengths), 3)]
        nf = int(rng.randint(1, 5))
        freq_pool = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
        freqs = []
        for k in range(nf):
            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(freq_pool)))
            freqs.append(freq_pool.pop(idx))
        amps = rng.uniform(0.3, 4.0, nf)
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, nf)
        dc = float(rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5))
        a = _make_exp_fourier(n, freqs, amps, phases, dc)
        s = _score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
            counter += 1

    if len(lengths) > 3:
        for r in range(12):
            n = lengths[3]
            nf = int(rng.randint(1, 4))
            freq_pool = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]
            freqs = []
            for k in range(nf):
                idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(freq_pool)))
                freqs.append(freq_pool.pop(idx))
            amps = rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0, nf)
            phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, nf)
            dc = float(rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5))
            a = _make_exp_fourier(n, freqs, amps, phases, dc)
            s = _score_array(a)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                pool.append((float(s), counter, a))
                counter += 1

    if not pool:
        a = _endpoint_tent(1024)
        pool.append((_score_array(a), 0, a))

    pool.sort()

    small = []
    mid = []
    large = []
    for item in pool:
        a = item[2]
        n = int(a.size)
        if n <= 1025:
            if len(small) < 8:
                small.append(a)
        elif n <= 2049:
            if len(mid) < 5:
                mid.append(a)
        elif n <= 4096:
            if len(large) < 3:
                large.append(a)

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf
    for item in pool[:8]:
        if np.isfinite(item[0]) and item[0] < best_score:
            best_score = item[0]
            best_a = item[2].copy()

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _endpoint_tent(1024)
        best_score = _score_array(best_a)

    print("[pool] best initial: n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(best_a.size), float(best_score)), flush=True)

    if small:
        res = _optimize_bucket(small, hard, reserve=260.0, max_count=6, base_alloc=130.0, seed=int(seed) + 1)
        for a_opt, s_opt in res:
            if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
                best_score = s_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                print("[improve] small n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    # Upsample the current best through larger lengths.
    up_targets = [2048, 3072, 4096]
    for target in up_targets:
        if best_a is None or target <= best_a.size:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() + 90.0 >= hard:
            break
        a_up = _resample(best_a, target)
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - 180.0
        if remaining <= 60.0:
            break
        alloc = max(30.0, min(180.0, remaining / 3.0))
        a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(a_up, time.monotonic() + alloc, seed=int(seed) + target)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
            best_score = s_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print("[improve] upsample n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    if mid:
        res = _optimize_bucket(mid, hard, reserve=240.0, max_count=4, base_alloc=160.0, seed=int(seed) + 101)
        for a_opt, s_opt in res:
            if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
                best_score = s_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                print("[improve] mid n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    for target in (3072, 4096):
        if best_a is None or target <= best_a.size:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() + 100.0 >= hard:
            break
        a_up = _resample(best_a, target)
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - 200.0
        if remaining <= 60.0:
            break
        alloc = max(30.0, min(180.0, remaining / 3.0))
        a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(a_up, time.monotonic() + alloc, seed=int(seed) + 9000 + target)
        if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
            best_score = s_opt
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            print("[improve] late upsample n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    if large:
        res = _optimize_bucket(large, hard, reserve=140.0, max_count=2, base_alloc=180.0, seed=int(seed) + 201)
        for a_opt, s_opt in res:
            if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
                best_score = s_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                print("[improve] large n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    if best_a is not None and best_a.size < 8192 and time.monotonic() + 180.0 < hard:
        a_up = _resample(best_a, 8192)
        remaining = hard - time.monotonic() - 130.0
        if remaining > 60.0:
            alloc = max(40.0, min(220.0, remaining / 2.0))
            a_opt, s_opt = _run_candidate(a_up, time.monotonic() + alloc, seed=int(seed) + 8192)
            if np.isfinite(s_opt) and s_opt < best_score:
                best_score = s_opt
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                print("[improve] 8192 n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_opt.size), float(s_opt)), flush=True)

    # Final local polishing.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < hard - 70.0:
        dl = min(hard - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 50.0)
        a_lp, s_lp = _lp_polish(best_a, dl, max_iters=5)
        if np.isfinite(s_lp) and s_lp < best_score:
            best_score = s_lp
            best_a = a_lp.copy()
            print("[improve] LP n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_lp.size), float(s_lp)), flush=True)

    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard - 80.0:
        dl = min(hard - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 140.0)
        a_hi, s_hi = _lbfqs_polish(best_a, dl, seed=int(seed) + 777)
        if np.isfinite(s_hi) and s_hi < best_score:
            best_score = s_hi
            best_a = a_hi.copy()
            print("[improve] high-beta n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_hi.size), float(s_hi)), flush=True)

    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < hard - 50.0:
        noise = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) + 999).standard_normal(best_a.size)
        pert = best_a + (0.005 * float(np.mean(best_a))) * noise
        pert = _normalize_sequence(np.clip(pert, 0.0, None))
        dl = min(hard - 25.0, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
        a_p, s_p = _lbfqs_polish(pert, dl, seed=int(seed) + 1009)
        if np.isfinite(s_p) and s_p < best_score:
            best_score = s_p
            best_a = a_p.copy()
            print("[improve] noise restart n=%d score=%.8f" % (int(a_p.size), float(s_p)), flush=True)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _endpoint_tent(1024)
        best_score = _score_array(best_a)

    # Choose between current best and a downsampled version if very long.
    final_candidates = [best_a]
    if best_a.size > 4096:
        final_candidates.append(_resample(best_a, 4096))
    if best_a.size != 1024 and best_a.size > 1024:
        final_candidates.append(_resample(best_a, 1024))

    chosen = final_candidates[0]
    chosen_score = _score_array(chosen)
    for cand in final_candidates[1:]:
        sc = _score_array(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
            chosen_score = sc
            chosen = cand

    # Remove numerical dust and normalize one final time.
    clean = np.asarray(chosen, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    clean[clean < 1e-15] = 0.0
    clean = _normalize_sequence(clean)

    out_list = _as_list(clean)
    if clean.size <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < hard - 2.0:
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                out_list = _as_list(_resample(clean, 1024))
        except Exception:
            pass

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("[final] elapsed=%.1fs score=%.9f length=%d" % (elapsed, float(chosen_score), int(clean.size)), flush=True)
    return out_list
```
